# Optimize nightly NPU test pipeline execution time

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/33169) · state: closed · merged: False

## Original PR description

## Summary
- Split single-node-poc into short/long parallel jobs using a 1.5h average-duration threshold
- Start multi-node tests right after the short job finishes, in parallel with the long job
- Add average-duration comments to long-running (>1.5h) test cases
- Refactor CPU test quantization constants (FP8_MAX/FP8_MIN/INT8_MAX etc.) into utils.py
